<small>[TensuraMoreSkills](../index.md) &rsaquo; [Configs](index.md)</small>

## `[obtainment]`

Obtainment settings for Witch of Vainglory.

| Option | Default | Range | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `acquirementMagiculeCost` | 500,000 | 0 to no limit | Magicule cost required to naturally acquire Abhoth. |
| `acquirementMastery` | 0 | -1 to no limit | Starting mastery value when acquired. |
| `requiredSkillId` | "tensuramoreskills:fungus_infection" |  | Required skill registry ID for obtainment. Empty string disables the required skill check. |
| `requiredSkillMustBeMastered` | true |  | If true, Fungus Infection must be mastered before Abhoth can be acquired. |
| `acquirementMagiculeCost` | 54,000 | 0 to no limit | Magicule cost required for natural obtainment of Albis Vina. |
| `acquirementMastery` | 0 | -1 to no limit | Starting mastery value when Albis Vina is acquired. |
| `maxMastery` | 1,000 | 1 to no limit | Maximum mastery value for Albis Vina. |
| `requiredSkillId` | "" |  | Skill registry ID required before Albis Vina can be naturally acquired. |
| `requiredSkillMustBeMastered` | false |  | If true, the required skill must be mastered before Albis Vina can be acquired. |
| `acquirementMagiculeCost` | 500,000 | 0 to no limit | Magicule cost required to naturally acquire Axiom. |
| `acquirementMastery` | 0 | -1 to no limit | Starting mastery when Axiom is obtained. |
| `maxMastery` | 4,500 | 1 to no limit | Maximum mastery for Axiom. |
| `requiredSkillId` | "tensura:reflector" |  | Required skill registry ID. Empty string disables the required skill check if the skill code supports it. |

| `acquirementMastery` | 0 | -1 to no limit | Starting mastery when Caedros is obtained. |

## `[formula_succession]`

| Option | Default | Range | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `formulaCloneCastingEnabled` | true |  | If true, Ars Weiss clones mirror the user's magic casts while Albis Vina is toggled. |
| `formulaAllowInstantMagic` | false |  | If true, clones may mirror instant-cast magic. Usually false is safer. |
| `formulaMaxCloneCastsPerPlayerCast` | 3 | 0 to 64 | Maximum number of Ars Weiss clones that may mirror one player magic cast. |
| `formulaCloneMagiculeCostMultiplier` | 0.35 | 0 to 100 | Magicule cost multiplier charged to the owner for each clone-cast magic. |
| `formulaCloneAuraCostMultiplier` | 0.35 | 0 to 100 | Aura cost multiplier charged to the owner for each clone-cast magic. |
| `formulaCloneCastPowerMultiplier` | 0.5 | 0 to 100 | Power multiplier for clone-cast magic when a custom damage hook supports it. |
| `formulaConsumesCloneOnCast` | false |  | If true, a clone shatters after finishing a mirrored magic cast. |
| `formulaDestroyCloneOnFailedCast` | false |  | If true, a clone shatters when its mirrored magic cast cannot be completed. |
| `formulaCloneExtraLifeTicks` | 100 | 0 to no limit | Extra lifetime given to a clone when it starts mirroring a magic cast. |
| `formulaCooldownTicks` | 12 | 0 to no limit | Legacy cooldown value for old interrupted-cast Formula Succession logic. |
| `formulaMinimumHeldTicks` | 5 | 0 to no limit | Legacy minimum held ticks for old interrupted-cast Formula Succession logic. |
| `formulaInterruptGraceTicks` | 10 | 1 to 20 | Legacy grace ticks for old interrupted-cast Formula Succession logic. |
| `formulaAllowStaffCasting` | false |  | If true, staff casting can be mirrored by Ars Weiss clones. |
| `formulaAllowAllMagic` | true |  | If true, Formula Succession can mirror any skill tagged as Tensura magic. |
| `formulaAllowSummoningMagic` | true |  | If true, summoning magic is allowed when Formula Succession is not restricted to all magic. |
| `formulaAllowNecromancyMagic` | true |  | If true, necromancy magic is allowed when Formula Succession is not restricted to all magic. |
| `formulaSuccessionBlacklist` | "tensura:megiddo", "tensura:spatial_storage", "tensura:warp_portal", "tensura:escape", "tensura:teleport", "tensura:gate" |  | Spell registry IDs that Formula Succession is not allowed to mirror. |

## `[avatar_beam_timing]`

Avatar Beam charge, duration, and cooldown values. 20 ticks = 1 second.

| Option | Default | Range | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `avatarBeamArmReleaseTicks` | 10 | 1 to no limit | Minimum hold time before releasing Avatar Beam is allowed. Default 10 = 0.5 seconds. |
| `avatarBeamMaxHoldTicks` | 100 | 1 to no limit | Hold time needed for a full-power Avatar Beam. Default 100 = 5 seconds. |
| `avatarBeamMinDurationTicks` | 100 | 1 to no limit | Beam duration at minimum charge. Default 100 = 5 seconds. |
| `avatarBeamMaxDurationTicks` | 220 | 1 to no limit | Beam duration at full charge. Default 220 = 11 seconds. |
| `avatarBeamCooldownTicks` | 90 | 0 to no limit | Cooldown after Avatar Beam releases. Default 90 = 4.5 seconds. |

## `[avatar_beam_ranges]`

Avatar Beam range and hitbox values.

| Option | Default | Range | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `avatarBeamTargetRange` | 220 | 1 to 4,096 | Range used to find the looked-at target for beam visuals. |
| `avatarBeamRange` | 260 | 1 to 4,096 | Actual beam length from the caster's aim direction. |
| `avatarBeamSendRange` | 420 | 1 to 4,096 | Network visibility range around the beam line. Higher values show the beam to farther players but send to more clients. |
| `avatarBeamRadius` | 20 | 0 to 512 | Main beam damage radius before charge scaling. Bigger values make the beam easier to hit with. |
| `avatarBeamFinalRadius` | 34 | 0 to 512 | Finisher pulse radius before charge scaling. This is used on release and final pulses. |
| `avatarBeamCoreRadius` | 9 | 0 to 512 | Inner beam core radius. Targets near the center take extra core damage. |

## `[avatar_beam_damage]`

Avatar Beam damage values.

| Option | Default | Range | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `avatarBeamTickDamage` | 85,000 | 0 to 340282349999999991754788743781432688640 | Base damage for repeated beam ticks before charge multiplier, distance bonus, and core bonus. |
| `avatarBeamFinalDamage` | 180,000 | 0 to 340282349999999991754788743781432688640 | Base damage for release/final pulses before charge multiplier, distance bonus, and core bonus. |
| `avatarBeamCoreDamage` | 260,000 | 0 to 340282349999999991754788743781432688640 | Extra damage added near the center of the beam. This rewards direct hits. |
| `avatarBeamSpiritualDamage` | 95,000 | 0 to 340282349999999991754788743781432688640 | Extra direct spiritual damage applied after normal space damage. This helps against high-health Tensura entities. |
| `avatarBeamForcedMinimumHealthDamage` | 80 | 0 to 340282349999999991754788743781432688640 | Fallback direct health damage if normal damage was fully resisted. Prevents the beam from doing literally nothing to extreme resistance targets. |

## `[avatar_beam_scaling]`

Avatar Beam charge scaling. Final multiplier = base + chargePercent \* bonus.

| Option | Default | Range | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `avatarBeamDamageMultiplierBase` | 2 | 0 to 1,024 | Damage multiplier at minimum release. Default 2.0 means 200 percent. |
| `avatarBeamDamageMultiplierBonus` | 8 | 0 to 1,024 | Extra damage multiplier gained at full charge. Default 8.0 means full charge becomes 10x total with base 2.0. |
| `avatarBeamRadiusMultiplierBase` | 0.95 | 0 to 1,024 | Radius multiplier at minimum release. |
| `avatarBeamRadiusMultiplierBonus` | 0.95 | 0 to 1,024 | Extra radius multiplier gained at full charge. |

## `[avatar_beam_intervals]`

Avatar Beam interval values. Higher intervals are generally better for performance.

| Option | Default | Range | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `avatarBeamPayloadRefreshTicks` | 4 | 1 to no limit | How often active beam visuals are resent to nearby players. Default 4 = five times per second. |
| `avatarBeamDamageIntervalTicks` | 2 | 1 to no limit | How often the active beam deals repeated damage. Default 2 = ten times per second. |
| `avatarBeamPulseIntervalTicks` | 16 | 1 to no limit | How often normal pulse damage/sounds happen during the beam. Default 16 = 0.8 seconds. |
| `avatarBeamFinisherPulseIntervalTicks` | 45 | 1 to no limit | How often stronger finisher pulse damage/sounds happen during the beam. Default 45 = 2.25 seconds. |
| `avatarBeamMasteryIntervalTicks` | 20 | 1 to no limit | How often holding the beam gives mastery. Default 20 = once per second. |
| `avatarBeamChargeMessageIntervalTicks` | 5 | 1 to no limit | How often the action-bar charge percentage updates while holding. |
| `avatarBeamChargeSoundIntervalTicks` | 10 | 1 to no limit | How often the charge sound plays while holding. |

## `[avatar_beam_degradation]`

Temporary resistance degradation applied to the caster while Avatar Beam is active.

| Option | Default | Range | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `avatarBeamResistanceDegradation` | 4 | 0 to 1,000 | General Resistance Degradation bonus while the beam is active. Higher values make enemies resist less. |
| `avatarBeamSpaceResistanceDegradation` | 4 | 0 to 1,000 | Space Resistance Degradation bonus while the beam is active. Higher values specifically strengthen space damage. |

## `[cube_judgement_costs]`

Cube Judgement costs. Normal cast drops one cube on a target. Shift cast creates a cube rain around the user.

| Option | Default | Range | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `cubeJudgementNormalCost` | 9,000 | 0 to no limit | Magicule cost for normal Cube Judgement. |
| `cubeJudgementShiftCost` | 18,500 | 0 to no limit | Magicule cost for shift Cube Judgement rain. |

## `[cube_judgement_activation]`

Cube Judgement activation and targeting.

| Option | Default | Range | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `cubeJudgementNormalCooldownTicks` | 42 | 0 to no limit | Cooldown after normal Cube Judgement. |
| `cubeJudgementShiftCooldownTicks` | 90 | 0 to no limit | Cooldown after shift Cube Judgement rain. |
| `cubeJudgementNormalMasteredCooldownReductionTicks` | 8 | 0 to no limit | Cooldown reduction for normal Cube Judgement when mastered. |
| `cubeJudgementShiftMasteredCooldownReductionTicks` | 14 | 0 to no limit | Cooldown reduction for shift Cube Judgement when mastered. |
| `cubeJudgementTargetRange` | 48 | 1 to 512 | Target range for normal Cube Judgement before mastery. |
| `cubeJudgementTargetRangeMastered` | 64 | 1 to 512 | Target range for normal Cube Judgement after mastery. |

## `[cube_judgement_timing]`

Cube Judgement lifetime and anti-lag limits.

| Option | Default | Range | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `cubeJudgementSingleFallDurationTicks` | 105 | 1 to no limit | Fall duration for the single cube. |
| `cubeJudgementRainFallDurationTicks` | 145 | 1 to no limit | Fall duration for rain cubes. |
| `cubeJudgementGroundDespawnTicks` | 20 | 0 to no limit | How long cubes remain after hitting solid ground. |
| `cubeJudgementNoGroundDespawnTicks` | 400 | 0 to no limit | How long cubes remain when no solid ground is found. |
| `cubeJudgementWatchRange` | 280 | 1 to 4,096 | Range used to send falling cube visuals to players. Higher values show farther but send to more clients. |
| `cubeJudgementRainCubes` | 34 | 1 to 512 | Number of cubes in shift rain before mastery. |
| `cubeJudgementRainCubesMastered` | 42 | 1 to 512 | Number of cubes in shift rain after mastery. |
| `cubeJudgementRainCubeDelayTicks` | 2 | 0 to no limit | Base delay added between each rain cube. |
| `cubeJudgementRainCubeDelayRandomTicks` | 8 | 0 to no limit | Random extra delay spread for rain cubes. |
| `cubeJudgementRainExtraLifetimeTicks` | 90 | 0 to no limit | Extra lifetime added after rain cube fall/despawn time. |
| `cubeJudgementSingleRecentHitTicks` | 12 | 0 to no limit | Same target cannot be hit again by the single cube inside this tick window. |
| `cubeJudgementRainRecentHitTicks` | 9 | 0 to no limit | Same target cannot be hit again by rain cubes inside this tick window. |
| `cubeJudgementRainMaxHitsPerTick` | 6 | 1 to no limit | Maximum rain hits processed per tick before mastery. Keeps rain from lagging massive crowds. |
| `cubeJudgementRainMaxHitsPerTickMastered` | 8 | 1 to no limit | Maximum rain hits processed per tick after mastery. |
| `cubeJudgementSingleMaxImpactTargets` | 10 | 1 to no limit | Maximum targets hit by the single cube impact burst. |
| `cubeJudgementRainMaxImpactTargets` | 4 | 1 to no limit | Maximum targets hit by each rain cube impact burst. |

## `[cube_judgement_shape]`

Cube Judgement cube sizes, rain spread, height, and falling sway.

| Option | Default | Range | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `cubeJudgementSingleSize` | 7.2 | 0.1 to 128 | Single cube size before mastery. |
| `cubeJudgementSingleSizeMastered` | 8.6 | 0.1 to 128 | Single cube size after mastery. |
| `cubeJudgementRainSize` | 4 | 0.1 to 128 | Base rain cube size before mastery. |
| `cubeJudgementRainSizeMastered` | 4.8 | 0.1 to 128 | Base rain cube size after mastery. |
| `cubeJudgementRainBigSizeBonus` | 1.5 | 0 to 128 | Extra size for every sixth large rain cube before mastery. |
| `cubeJudgementRainBigSizeBonusMastered` | 2 | 0 to 128 | Extra size for every sixth large rain cube after mastery. |
| `cubeJudgementRainMediumSizeBonus` | 1 | 0 to 128 | Extra size for medium rain cubes before mastery. |
| `cubeJudgementRainMediumSizeBonusMastered` | 1.4 | 0 to 128 | Extra size for medium rain cubes after mastery. |
| `cubeJudgementRainSmallSizeBonus` | 0.7 | 0 to 128 | Extra size for small bonus rain cubes. |
| `cubeJudgementRainRadius` | 25 | 0 to 512 | Rain spread radius before mastery. |
| `cubeJudgementRainRadiusMastered` | 31 | 0 to 512 | Rain spread radius after mastery. |
| `cubeJudgementSingleStartHeight` | 41 | 0 to 512 | Single cube start height before mastery. |
| `cubeJudgementSingleStartHeightMastered` | 49 | 0 to 512 | Single cube start height after mastery. |
| `cubeJudgementRainStartHeight` | 36 | 0 to 512 | Rain cube start height before mastery. |
| `cubeJudgementRainStartHeightMastered` | 43 | 0 to 512 | Rain cube start height after mastery. |
| `cubeJudgementSingleFallbackEndYOffset` | 1.75 | -512 to 512 | Single cube fallback end Y offset when no solid ground is found before mastery. |
| `cubeJudgementSingleFallbackEndYOffsetMastered` | 2.25 | -512 to 512 | Single cube fallback end Y offset when no solid ground is found after mastery. |
| `cubeJudgementRainFallbackEndYOffset` | 1.25 | -512 to 512 | Rain cube fallback end Y offset when no solid ground is found before mastery. |
| `cubeJudgementRainFallbackEndYOffsetMastered` | 1.55 | -512 to 512 | Rain cube fallback end Y offset when no solid ground is found after mastery. |
| `cubeJudgementSingleSway` | 0.65 | 0 to 128 | Horizontal falling sway for single cubes. |
| `cubeJudgementRainSway` | 1.55 | 0 to 128 | Horizontal falling sway for rain cubes. |

## `[cube_judgement_damage]`

Cube Judgement damage values.

| Option | Default | Range | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `cubeJudgementSingleDamage` | 310 | 0 to 340282349999999991754788743781432688640 | Flat damage for single cube before mastery. |

| `onLearnMagiculeDrain` | 2,000,000 | 0 to no limit | Magicules removed once when Kronairos is learned permanently. |

## `[asmoday.costs]`

Magicule costs for Asmoday modes. These are only the activation/hold costs used by each mode, not obtainment costs.

| Option | Default | Range | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `spaceEmbodimentCost` | 50,000 | 0 to no limit | Magicule cost for Space Embodiment. This mode is Asmoday's defensive/spatial state. |
| `spatialCubeBarrageCost` | 65,000 | 0 to no limit | Magicule cost for Spatial Cube Barrage. This mode fires cube projectiles. |
| `cubeJudgementCost` | 90,000 | 0 to no limit | Magicule cost for Cube Judgement. This mode calls down the falling spatial judgement cube. |
| `prisonRealmCost` | 120,000 | 0 to no limit | Magicule cost for Prison Realm. This mode traps the target inside Asmoday's prison logic. |
| `endBlackHoleCost` | 150,000 | 0 to no limit | Magicule cost for End Black Hole. This mode creates the black hole absorption attack. |
| `avatarBeamCost` | 120,000 | 0 to no limit | Magicule cost for Avatar Beam. This is checked when the beam starts charging/releasing. |

## `[asmoday.avatar_beam_timing]`

Avatar Beam charge, duration, and cooldown values. 20 ticks = 1 second.

| Option | Default | Range | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `avatarBeamArmReleaseTicks` | 10 | 1 to no limit | Minimum hold time before releasing Avatar Beam is allowed. Default 10 = 0.5 seconds. |
| `avatarBeamMaxHoldTicks` | 100 | 1 to no limit | Hold time needed for a full-power Avatar Beam. Default 100 = 5 seconds. |
| `avatarBeamMinDurationTicks` | 100 | 1 to no limit | Beam duration at minimum charge. Default 100 = 5 seconds. |
| `avatarBeamMaxDurationTicks` | 220 | 1 to no limit | Beam duration at full charge. Default 220 = 11 seconds. |
| `avatarBeamCooldownTicks` | 90 | 0 to no limit | Cooldown after Avatar Beam releases. Default 90 = 4.5 seconds. |

## `[asmoday.avatar_beam_ranges]`

Avatar Beam range and hitbox values.

| Option | Default | Range | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `avatarBeamTargetRange` | 220 | 1 to 4,096 | Range used to find the looked-at target for beam visuals. |
| `avatarBeamRange` | 260 | 1 to 4,096 | Actual beam length from the caster's aim direction. |
| `avatarBeamSendRange` | 420 | 1 to 4,096 | Network visibility range around the beam line. Higher values show the beam to farther players but send to more clients. |
| `avatarBeamRadius` | 20 | 0 to 512 | Main beam damage radius before charge scaling. Bigger values make the beam easier to hit with. |
| `avatarBeamFinalRadius` | 34 | 0 to 512 | Finisher pulse radius before charge scaling. This is used on release and final pulses. |
| `avatarBeamCoreRadius` | 9 | 0 to 512 | Inner beam core radius. Targets near the center take extra core damage. |

## `[asmoday.avatar_beam_damage]`

Avatar Beam damage values.

| Option | Default | Range | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `avatarBeamTickDamage` | 85,000 | 0 to 340282349999999991754788743781432688640 | Base damage for repeated beam ticks before charge multiplier, distance bonus, and core bonus. |
| `avatarBeamFinalDamage` | 180,000 | 0 to 340282349999999991754788743781432688640 | Base damage for release/final pulses before charge multiplier, distance bonus, and core bonus. |
| `avatarBeamCoreDamage` | 260,000 | 0 to 340282349999999991754788743781432688640 | Extra damage added near the center of the beam. This rewards direct hits. |
| `avatarBeamSpiritualDamage` | 95,000 | 0 to 340282349999999991754788743781432688640 | Extra direct spiritual damage applied after normal space damage. This helps against high-health Tensura entities. |
| `avatarBeamForcedMinimumHealthDamage` | 80 | 0 to 340282349999999991754788743781432688640 | Fallback direct health damage if normal damage was fully resisted. Prevents the beam from doing literally nothing to extreme resistance targets. |

## `[asmoday.avatar_beam_scaling]`

Avatar Beam charge scaling. Final multiplier = base + chargePercent \* bonus.

| Option | Default | Range | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `avatarBeamDamageMultiplierBase` | 2 | 0 to 1,024 | Damage multiplier at minimum release. Default 2.0 means 200 percent. |
| `avatarBeamDamageMultiplierBonus` | 8 | 0 to 1,024 | Extra damage multiplier gained at full charge. Default 8.0 means full charge becomes 10x total with base 2.0. |
| `avatarBeamRadiusMultiplierBase` | 0.95 | 0 to 1,024 | Radius multiplier at minimum release. |
| `avatarBeamRadiusMultiplierBonus` | 0.95 | 0 to 1,024 | Extra radius multiplier gained at full charge. |

## `[asmoday.avatar_beam_intervals]`

Avatar Beam interval values. Higher intervals are generally better for performance.

| Option | Default | Range | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `avatarBeamPayloadRefreshTicks` | 4 | 1 to no limit | How often active beam visuals are resent to nearby players. Default 4 = five times per second. |
| `avatarBeamDamageIntervalTicks` | 2 | 1 to no limit | How often the active beam deals repeated damage. Default 2 = ten times per second. |
| `avatarBeamPulseIntervalTicks` | 16 | 1 to no limit | How often normal pulse damage/sounds happen during the beam. Default 16 = 0.8 seconds. |
| `avatarBeamFinisherPulseIntervalTicks` | 45 | 1 to no limit | How often stronger finisher pulse damage/sounds happen during the beam. Default 45 = 2.25 seconds. |
| `avatarBeamMasteryIntervalTicks` | 20 | 1 to no limit | How often holding the beam gives mastery. Default 20 = once per second. |
| `avatarBeamChargeMessageIntervalTicks` | 5 | 1 to no limit | How often the action-bar charge percentage updates while holding. |
| `avatarBeamChargeSoundIntervalTicks` | 10 | 1 to no limit | How often the charge sound plays while holding. |

## `[asmoday.avatar_beam_degradation]`

Temporary resistance degradation applied to the caster while Avatar Beam is active.

| Option | Default | Range | Description |
|---|---|---|---|

| `avatarBeamSpaceResistanceDegradation` | 4 | 0 to 1,000 | Space Resistance Degradation bonus while the beam is active. Higher values specifically strengthen space damage. |

## `[albis_vina.formula_succession]`

| Option | Default | Range | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `formulaCloneCastingEnabled` | true |  | If true, Ars Weiss clones mirror the user's magic casts while Albis Vina is toggled. |
| `formulaAllowInstantMagic` | false |  | If true, clones may mirror instant-cast magic. Usually false is safer. |
| `formulaMaxCloneCastsPerPlayerCast` | 3 | 0 to 64 | Maximum number of Ars Weiss clones that may mirror one player magic cast. |
| `formulaCloneMagiculeCostMultiplier` | 0.35 | 0 to 100 | Magicule cost multiplier charged to the owner for each clone-cast magic. |
| `formulaCloneAuraCostMultiplier` | 0.35 | 0 to 100 | Aura cost multiplier charged to the owner for each clone-cast magic. |
| `formulaCloneCastPowerMultiplier` | 0.5 | 0 to 100 | Power multiplier for clone-cast magic when a custom damage hook supports it. |
| `formulaConsumesCloneOnCast` | false |  | If true, a clone shatters after finishing a mirrored magic cast. |
| `formulaDestroyCloneOnFailedCast` | false |  | If true, a clone shatters when its mirrored magic cast cannot be completed. |
| `formulaCloneExtraLifeTicks` | 100 | 0 to no limit | Extra lifetime given to a clone when it starts mirroring a magic cast. |
| `formulaCooldownTicks` | 12 | 0 to no limit | Legacy cooldown value for old interrupted-cast Formula Succession logic. |
| `formulaMinimumHeldTicks` | 5 | 0 to no limit | Legacy minimum held ticks for old interrupted-cast Formula Succession logic. |
| `formulaInterruptGraceTicks` | 10 | 1 to 20 | Legacy grace ticks for old interrupted-cast Formula Succession logic. |
| `formulaAllowStaffCasting` | false |  | If true, staff casting can be mirrored by Ars Weiss clones. |
| `formulaAllowAllMagic` | true |  | If true, Formula Succession can mirror any skill tagged as Tensura magic. |
| `formulaAllowSummoningMagic` | true |  | If true, summoning magic is allowed when Formula Succession is not restricted to all magic. |
| `formulaAllowNecromancyMagic` | true |  | If true, necromancy magic is allowed when Formula Succession is not restricted to all magic. |
| `formulaSuccessionBlacklist` | "tensura:megiddo", "tensura:spatial_storage", "tensura:warp_portal", "tensura:escape", "tensura:teleport", "tensura:gate" |  | Spell registry IDs that Formula Succession is not allowed to mirror. |

| `holdArmTicks` | 20 | 0 to 72,000 | Ticks held before Lionheart activates. 20 ticks = 1 second. |
| `preventionFreeTicks` | 200 | 0 to 72,000 | Safe Lionheart time. Default 200 ticks = 10 seconds. |
| `lionheartOvertimeHpPercentPerSecond` | 0.1 | 0 to 1 | Self severance HP damage per second after the safe window. 0.10 = 10% max HP per second. |
| `baseCooldownTicks` | 10 | 0 to 72,000 | Base Lionheart cooldown after release. Default 200 ticks = 10 seconds. |
| `extraCooldownPerOverSecondTicks` | 10 | 0 to 72,000 | Extra cooldown per second held over the safe window. |
| `penaltyDurationTicks` | 6,000 | 1 to 72,000 | How long Lionheart overtime HP penalty lasts after being applied. Default 6000 ticks = 5 minutes. |

| `penaltyDurationTicks` | 6,000 | 1 to 72,000 | How long Lionheart overtime HP penalty lasts after being applied. Default 6000 ticks = 5 minutes. |

Area and range values.

## `[reality_change]`

Reality Change balance values.

| Option | Default | Range | Description |
|---|---|---|---|

| `realityChangeConfusionTicks` | 80 | 1 to no limit | Confusion duration applied by Reality Change. |
| `realityChangeConfusionAmplifier` | 0 | 0 to 255 | Confusion amplifier applied by Reality Change. 0 means level 1. |

| `realityCoatStrengthAmplifier` | 1 | 0 to 255 | Strength amplifier during Reality Coat. 0 means level 1. |
| `realityCoatSpeedAmplifier` | 1 | 0 to 255 | Speed amplifier during Reality Coat. 0 means level 1. |
| `realityCoatRegenerationAmplifier` | 1 | 0 to 255 | Self Regeneration amplifier during Reality Coat. 0 means level 1. |
| `realityCoatEffectDurationTicks` | 100 | 1 to no limit | Duration of each refreshed Reality Coat effect application. |
| `realityCoatEffectRefreshIntervalTicks` | 80 | 1 to no limit | How often Reality Coat refreshes its buffs. Higher is better for performance. |

Deleted Timeline counter and projectile reversal values.

| `counterReflectMultiplier` | 1.75 | 0 to 1,024 | Damage reflection multiplier. Reflected damage = incoming damage multiplied by this value. |
| `counterReflectMinimumDamage` | 1 | 0 to 340282349999999991754788743781432688640 | Minimum reflected damage dealt by Deleted Timeline. |
| `projectileReverseMinimumSpeed` | 1.5 | 0 to 128 | Minimum speed given to a reversed projectile. |
| `projectileReverseFallbackSpeed` | 1.5 | 0 to 128 | Speed used when a projectile has almost no movement and needs a fallback direction. |

| `falsePerceptionBlindnessAmplifier` | 0 | 0 to 255 | Flashed Blindness amplifier. 0 means level 1. |
| `falsePerceptionConfusionAmplifier` | 0 | 0 to 255 | Confusion amplifier. 0 means level 1. |
| `falsePerceptionMovementInterferenceAmplifier` | 0 | 0 to 255 | Movement Interference amplifier. 0 means level 1. |

## `[rewritten_death]`

Death rewrite revive values.

| Option | Default | Range | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `maxRevivesPerDay` | 3 | 0 to no limit | Maximum number of death rewrites per Minecraft day. |
| `reviveHealthRatio` | 0.6 | 0 to 1 | Health restored on revive as a percentage of max health. 0.6 = 60%. |
| `reviveMinimumHealth` | 20 | 0 to 340282349999999991754788743781432688640 | Minimum health restored by revive, even if the percentage would be lower. |
| `reviveInvulnerabilityTicks` | 80 | 0 to no limit | Invulnerability ticks after revive. |
| `reviveResistanceTicks` | 100 | 1 to no limit | Resistance duration after revive. |
| `reviveResistanceAmplifier` | 4 | 0 to 255 | Resistance amplifier after revive. 0 means level 1. |
| `reviveRegenerationTicks` | 200 | 1 to no limit | Self Regeneration duration after revive. |
| `reviveRegenerationAmplifier` | 1 | 0 to 255 | Self Regeneration amplifier after revive. 0 means level 1. |
| `reviveHakiCoatTicks` | 120 | 1 to no limit | Haki Coat duration after revive. |
| `reviveHakiCoatAmplifier` | 0 | 0 to 255 | Haki Coat amplifier after revive. 0 means level 1. |
| `reviveSpiritualHealthRatio` | 0.5 | 0 to 1 | Minimum spiritual health restored on revive as a percentage of max spiritual health. |
| `reviveAuraRatio` | 0.4 | 0 to 1 | Minimum aura restored on revive as a percentage of max aura. |
| `reviveMagiculeRatio` | 0.4 | 0 to 1 | Minimum magicules restored on revive as a percentage of max magicules. |
